Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the diff_geom object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 617. The moduli space has dimension at most 699 with 1 connected components.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by diff_geom generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 617, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 699. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on diff_geom structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 366. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 303 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 366 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the diff_geom structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 303. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
